SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties

- *.jks
- *.keystore

Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     6	import android.os.Build

    11	import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
    12	import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
    13	import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
    14	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.CinerificApp
    15	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.theme.CinerificTheme

    17	class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    18	    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    19	        val bootStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
    20	        requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_FULL_SENSOR
    21	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
    22	            getSystemService(UiModeManager::class.java).setApplicationNightMode(
    23	                UiModeManager.MODE_NIGHT_YES
    24	            )
    25	        }
    26	        enableEdgeToEdge(
    27	            statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.TRANSPARENT),
    28	            navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.BLACK),
    29	        )
    30	        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    31	        window.statusBarColor = Color.TRANSPARENT
    32	        window.navigationBarColor = Color.BLACK
    33	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
    34	            window.navigationBarDividerColor = Color.BLACK
    35	        }
    36	        WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, window.decorView).apply {
    37	            isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
    38	            isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = false
    39	        }
    40	        setContent {
    41	            CinerificTheme {
    42	                CinerificApp(bootStartMillis = bootStartMillis)
    43	            }
    44	        }
    45	    }
    46	}

   123	@Composable
   124	fun CinerificApp(bootStartMillis: Long = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) {
   125	    val introBootStartMillis by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(bootStartMillis) }
   126	    var showHome by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   127	    var signedInProfile by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificProfile.Guest) }
   128	    var signInSessionId by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(0) }
   129	    var selectedLanguage by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificLanguage.English) }
   130	    var introSnapshot by rememberSaveable(stateSaver = CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver) {
   131	        mutableStateOf(CinerificIntroSnapshot())
   132	    }
   133	
   134	    if (showHome) {
   135	        CinerificLocalizedResources(selectedLanguage) {
   136	            CinerificMainExperience(
   137	                signInSessionId = signInSessionId,
   138	                signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   139	                selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   140	                onLanguageSelected = { selectedLanguage = it },
   141	                onSignOut = {
   142	                    signedInProfile = CinerificProfile.Guest
   143	                    introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   144	                    showHome = false
   145	                }
   146	            )
   147	        }
   148	    } else {
   149	        AndroidView(
   150	            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
   151	            factory = { context ->
   152	                CinerificIntroView(context).apply {
   153	                    this.bootStartMillis = introBootStartMillis
   154	                    onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   155	                        if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   156	                            introSnapshot = snapshot
   157	                        }
   158	                    }
   159	                    restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   160	                    onAvatarSelected = { profile ->
   161	                        introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   162	                        signedInProfile = profile
   163	                        signInSessionId += 1
   164	                        showHome = true
   165	                    }
   166	                }
   167	            },
   168	            update = { view ->
   169	                view.bootStartMillis = introBootStartMillis
   170	                view.onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   171	                    if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   172	                        introSnapshot = snapshot
   173	                    }
   174	                }
   175	                view.restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   176	                view.onAvatarSelected = { profile ->
   177	                    introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   178	                    signedInProfile = profile
   179	                    signInSessionId += 1
   180	                    showHome = true
   181	                }
   182	            }
   183	        )
   184	    }
   185	}
   186	
   187	internal enum class CinerificDestination {
   188	    Home,
   189	    Movies,
   190	    Shows,
   191	    Favorites,
   192	    Settings,
   193	    ProgramDetails
   194	}
   195	
   196	internal enum class CinerificProfile(
   197	    @DrawableRes val avatarResId: Int,
   198	    @DrawableRes val nameResId: Int
   199	) {
   200	    Steve(R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.steve_name),
   201	    Martin(R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.martin_name),
   202	    Janny(R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.janny_name),
   203	    Guest(R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.guest_name)
   204	}
   205	
   206	@Composable
   207	private fun CinerificMainExperience(
   208	    signInSessionId: Int,
   209	    signedInProfile: CinerificProfile,
   210	    selectedLanguage: CinerificLanguage,
   211	    onLanguageSelected: (CinerificLanguage) -> Unit,
   212	    onSignOut: () -> Unit
   213	) {
   214	    var destination by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home) }
   215	    var selectedProgramTitle by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("Sink or Swim") }
   216	    var favoriteProgramTitles by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyList<String>()) }
   217	    var userProgramRatings by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyMap<String, Int>()) }
   218	    var catalogRouteDestinationName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   219	    var catalogRouteGenreName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   220	    var catalogRouteModeName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   221	    var favoritesFullPromptRequestId by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
   222	    var autoLogoutEnabled by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   223	    var userInitiatedPlaybackActive by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   224	    var lastInteractionMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) }
   225	    val playbackSessionController = remember(userInitiatedPlaybackActive) {
   226	        CinerificPlaybackSessionController(
   227	            isUserInitiatedPlaybackActive = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   228	            onUserInitiatedPlaybackStarted = {
   229	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = true
   230	            },
   231	            finishUserInitiatedPlayback = {
   232	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = false
   233	                lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   234	            }
   235	        )
   236	    }
   237	    val catalogRoute = remember(
   238	        catalogRouteDestinationName,
   239	        catalogRouteGenreName,
   240	        catalogRouteModeName
   241	    ) {
   242	        cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   243	            destinationName = catalogRouteDestinationName,
   244	            genreName = catalogRouteGenreName,
   245	            modeName = catalogRouteModeName
   246	        )
   247	    }
   248	
   249	    fun clearCatalogRoute() {
   250	        catalogRouteDestinationName = ""
   251	        catalogRouteGenreName = ""
   252	        catalogRouteModeName = ""
   253	    }
   254	
   255	    LaunchedEffect(autoLogoutEnabled, userInitiatedPlaybackActive, lastInteractionMillis) {
   256	        if (!autoLogoutEnabled || userInitiatedPlaybackActive) return@LaunchedEffect
   257	        delay(AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS)
   258	        val inactiveForMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - lastInteractionMillis
   259	        if (
   260	            autoLogoutEnabled &&
   261	            !userInitiatedPlaybackActive &&
   262	            inactiveForMillis >= AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS
   263	        ) {
   264	            onSignOut()
   265	        }
   266	    }
   267	
   268	    fun showProgramDetails(title: String) {
   269	        clearCatalogRoute()
   270	        selectedProgramTitle = title
   271	        destination = CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails
   272	    }
   273	
   274	    fun showCatalog(route: CinerificCatalogRoute) {
   275	        catalogRouteDestinationName = route.destination.name
   276	        catalogRouteGenreName = route.genre.name
   277	        catalogRouteModeName = route.mode.name
   278	        destination = route.destination
   279	    }
   280	
   281	    fun rateProgram(title: String, rating: Int) {
   282	        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings + (title to rating.coerceIn(1, 5))
   283	    }
   284	
   285	    fun toggleFavoriteProgram(title: String) {
   286	        if (title in favoriteProgramTitles) {
   287	            favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles - title
   288	            return
   289	        }
   290	
   291	        val isShow = cinerificProgramIsShow(title)
   292	        val filledSlots = cinerificFilledFavoritePlaceholderCount(
   293	            titles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   294	            isShow = isShow
   295	        )
   296	        if (filledSlots >= CINERIFIC_FAVORITES_CAPACITY) {
   297	            favoritesFullPromptRequestId += 1
   298	            return
   299	        }
   300	
   301	        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles + title
   302	    }
   303	
   304	    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalCinerificPlaybackSessionController provides playbackSessionController) {
   305	        Box(
   306	            modifier = Modifier
   307	                .fillMaxSize()
   308	                .pointerInput(Unit) {
   309	                    awaitPointerEventScope {
   310	                        while (true) {
   311	                            awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
   312	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   313	                        }
   314	                    }
   315	                }
   316	        ) {
   317	            when (destination) {
   318	                CinerificDestination.Home -> CinerificHomeScreen(
   319	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   320	                    onCatalogSelected = ::showCatalog,
   321	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   322	                )
   323	                CinerificDestination.Movies,
   324	                CinerificDestination.Shows,
   325	                CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   326	                CinerificDestination.Settings -> CinerificDestinationScreen(
   327	                    destination = destination,
   328	                    signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   329	                    selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   330	                    onLanguageSelected = onLanguageSelected,
   331	                    autoLogoutEnabled = autoLogoutEnabled,
   332	                    onAutoLogoutEnabledChange = { enabled ->
   333	                        autoLogoutEnabled = enabled
   334	                        lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   335	                    },
   336	                    onSignOut = onSignOut,
   337	                    favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   338	                    onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   339	                    userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   340	                    onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   341	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   342	                    catalogRoute = catalogRoute?.takeIf { it.destination == destination },
   343	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   344	                )
   345	                CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> CinerificProgramDetailsScreen(
   346	                    programTitle = selectedProgramTitle,
   347	                    favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   348	                    onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   349	                    userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   350	                    onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   351	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   352	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   353	                )
   354	            }
   355	
   356	            CinerificRightSideNavBar(
   357	                currentDestination = destination,
   358	                onDestinationSelected = {
   359	                    clearCatalogRoute()
   360	                    destination = it
   361	                },
   362	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   363	            )
   364	
   365	            FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   366	                requestId = favoritesFullPromptRequestId,
   367	                currentDestination = destination,
   368	                suppressed = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   369	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   370	            )
   371	        }
   372	    }
   373	}
   374	
   375	private fun cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   376	    destinationName: String,
   377	    genreName: String,
   378	    modeName: String
   379	): CinerificCatalogRoute? {
   380	    if (destinationName.isBlank() || genreName.isBlank() || modeName.isBlank()) return null
   381	
   382	    val destination = CinerificDestination.values().firstOrNull { it.name == destinationName } ?: return null
   383	    val genre = ViewportGenre.values().firstOrNull { it.name == genreName } ?: return null
   384	    val mode = ViewportMode.values().firstOrNull { it.name == modeName } ?: return null
   385	
   386	    return CinerificCatalogRoute(
   387	        destination = destination,
   388	        genre = genre,
   389	        mode = mode
   390	    )
   391	}
   392	
   393	@Composable
   394	private fun FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   395	    requestId: Int,
   396	    currentDestination: CinerificDestination,
   397	    suppressed: Boolean,
   398	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   399	) {
   400	    var visible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   401	
   402	    LaunchedEffect(requestId, suppressed) {
   403	        if (suppressed) {
   404	            visible = false
   405	            return@LaunchedEffect
   406	        }
   407	        if (requestId == 0) {
   408	            visible = false
   409	            return@LaunchedEffect
   410	        }
   411	        visible = true
   412	        delay(FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_VISIBLE_MS)
   413	        visible = false
   414	    }
   415	
   416	    val alpha by animateFloatAsState(
   417	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0f,
   418	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   419	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-alpha"
   420	    )
   421	    val promptScale by animateFloatAsState(
   422	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0.94f,
   423	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   424	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-scale"
   425	    )
   426	
   427	    if (suppressed || requestId == 0 || (!visible && alpha <= 0.01f)) return
   428	
   429	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = modifier) {
   430	        val stageScale = maxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
   431	        val isPortraitDetail = currentDestination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails &&
   432	            maxHeight > maxWidth
   433	        val promptY = if (isPortraitDetail) {
   434	            DETAIL_HERO_LOGO_CENTER_Y - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT / 2f
   435	        } else {
   436	            FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_Y
   437	        }
   438	        FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   439	            stageScale = stageScale,
   440	            modifier = Modifier
   441	                .absoluteOffset(
   442	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_X * stageScale).dp,
   443	                    y = (promptY * stageScale).dp
   444	                )
   445	                .graphicsLayer {
   446	                    this.alpha = alpha
   447	                    scaleX = promptScale
   448	                    scaleY = promptScale
   449	                    transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0.5f, 0f)
   450	                }
   451	        )
   452	    }
   453	}
   454	
   455	@Composable
   456	private fun FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   457	    stageScale: Float,
   458	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   459	) {
   460	    Box(
   461	        modifier = modifier.requiredSize(
   462	            width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   463	            height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   464	        )
   465	    ) {
   466	        Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   467	            val xScale = size.width / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH
   468	            val yScale = size.height / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT
   469	            val pointer = Path().apply {
   470	                moveTo(47.5f * xScale, 0f)
   471	                lineTo(63f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   472	                lineTo(32f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   473	                close()
   474	            }
   475	            drawPath(pointer, ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent)
   476	            drawRoundRect(
   477	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptFill,
   478	                topLeft = Offset(0f, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale),
   479	                size = Size(
   480	                    width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * xScale,
   481	                    height = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT * yScale
   482	                ),
   483	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   484	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   485	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   486	                )
   487	            )
   488	            drawRoundRect(
   489	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   490	                topLeft = Offset(
   491	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * xScale / 2f,
   492	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale +
   493	                        FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * yScale / 2f
   494	                ),
   495	                size = Size(
   496	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * xScale,
   497	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * yScale
   498	                ),
   499	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   500	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   501	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   502	                ),
   503	                style = Stroke(width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * min(xScale, yScale))
   504	            )
   505	        }
   506	        Text(
   507	            text = "You've\nfilled\nup on\nfavorites!",
   508	            color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   509	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   510	            fontSize = (14f * stageScale).sp,
   511	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   512	            lineHeight = (21.252f * stageScale).sp,
   513	            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
   514	            modifier = Modifier
   515	                .absoluteOffset(
   516	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_X * stageScale).dp,
   517	                    y = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_Y * stageScale).dp
   518	                )
   519	                .requiredSize(
   520	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   521	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   522	                )
   523	        )
   524	    }
   525	}
   526	
   527	@Composable
   528	private fun BootIntroFromFigma(progress: Float) {
   529	    val p = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   530	    val images = rememberIntroImages()
   531	    val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   532	        p,
   533	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   534	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   535	    )
   536	    val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(p, 2500, 3900)
   537	    val background = introBackgroundBrush(
   538	        solidProgress = blackToPurple,
   539	        gradientProgress = purpleToSettle
   540	    )
   541	    val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(p, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   542	    val logoEntryProgress = bouncySegmentMs(p, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
   543	    val logoFinalProgress = easedSegmentMs(p, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   544	    val avatarSettleAlpha = easedSegmentMs(p, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   545	    val avatarYOffset = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarSettleAlpha)
   546	
   547	    Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   548	        val stageScale = min(size.width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, size.height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
   549	        val stageLeft = (size.width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f
   550	        val stageTop = (size.height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
   551	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(size.width > size.height)
   552	
   553	        drawRect(brush = background, size = size)
   554	
   555	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   556	            val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
   557	            val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
   558	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   559	                end = baseBounds,
   560	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   561	            )
   562	            val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
   563	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   564	                end = baseBounds,
   565	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   566	            )
   567	            val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
   568	
   569	            drawFigmaImage(
   570	                image = images.logoSimple,
   571	                bounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   572	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   573	                stageTop = stageTop,
   574	                stageScale = stageScale,
   575	                alpha = logoAlpha
   576	            )
   577	            drawFigmaImage(
   578	                image = images.logoEyes,
   579	                bounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   580	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   581	                stageTop = stageTop,
   582	                stageScale = stageScale,
   583	                alpha = logoAlpha
   584	            )
   585	        }
   586	
   587	        if (avatarSettleAlpha > 0.01f) {
   588	            val y = avatarYOffset
   589	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveAvatar, FigmaBounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   590	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinAvatar, FigmaBounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   591	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyAvatar, FigmaBounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   592	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestAvatar, FigmaBounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   593	
   594	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveName, FigmaBounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   595	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinName, FigmaBounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   596	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyName, FigmaBounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   597	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestName, FigmaBounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   598	        }
   599	    }
   600	}

   608	@Composable
   609	private fun IntroFrame2FromFigma() {
   610	    // Node 474:7311 with Logo Slide 1 instance 478:6917.
   611	    val slideYOffset = remember { Animatable(-417f) }
   612	
   613	    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
   614	        slideYOffset.snapTo(-417f)
   615	        slideYOffset.animateTo(
   616	            targetValue = 0f,
   617	            animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 1800, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing)
   618	        )
   619	    }
   620	
   621	    FigmaStage(background = solidBrush(ColorFrame2Background)) { scale ->
   622	        LogoSlide1Layer(scale = scale, yOffset = slideYOffset.value)
   623	    }
   624	}

   645	    // Node 478:6975 + Star Overlay 482:7696.

   648	            scale = scale,

   651	        )

   654	            logoResId = R.drawable.logo_simple_signin,
   655	            eyesResId = R.drawable.logo_eyes_signin
   656	        )
   657

   659	        Box(
   660	            modifier = Modifier
   661	                .absoluteOffset(x = figma(894f, scale), y = figma(266f, scale))
   662	                .requiredSize(figma(200f, scale), figma(200f, scale))
   663	                .clip(CircleShape)
   664	                .background(
   665	                    Brush.radialGradient(
   666	                        colors = listOf(Color(0xFFE8E8E8), Color(0xFF8A8A8A), Color(0xFF1D1D1D))
   667	                    )
   668	                )
   669	        )
   670	
   671	        // Node 485:7113 loading spinner instance, rebuilt from centered standalone layers.
   672	        val spinnerHeight = 300f *
   673	            CINERIFIC_LOADING_SPINNER_CANVAS_HEIGHT /
   674	            CINERIFIC_LOADING_SPINNER_CANVAS_WIDTH
   675	        CinerificLoadingSpinner(
   676	            modifier = Modifier
   677	                .absoluteOffset(
   678	                    x = figma(447f, scale),
   679	                    y = figma(267f - (spinnerHeight - 300f) / 2f, scale)
   680	                )
   681	                .requiredSize(figma(300f, scale), figma(spinnerHeight, scale))
   682	        )
   683	    }
   684	}

   773	)

   775	@Composable
   776	private fun rememberIntroImages(): IntroImages {
   777	    val resources = LocalContext.current.resources
   778	    return remember(resources) {
   779	        IntroImages(
   780	            logoSimple = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.logo_simple_large).asImageBitmap(),
   781	            logoEyes = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.logo_eyes_large).asImageBitmap(),
   782	            steveAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test).asImageBitmap(),
   783	            martinAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test).asImageBitmap(),
   784	            jannyAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test).asImageBitmap(),
   785	            guestAvatar = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test).asImageBitmap(),
   786	            steveName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.steve_name).asImageBitmap(),
   787	            martinName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.martin_name).asImageBitmap(),
   788	            jannyName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.janny_name).asImageBitmap(),
   789	            guestName = BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, R.drawable.guest_name).asImageBitmap()
   790	        )
   791	    }

   793	
   794	private fun DrawScope.drawFigmaImage(
   795	    image: ImageBitmap,
   796	    bounds: FigmaBounds,
   797	    stageLeft: Float,
   798	    stageTop: Float,
   799	    stageScale: Float,
   800	    alpha: Float = 1f,
   801	    clipCircle: Boolean = false
   802	) {
   803	    val left = stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale
   804	    val top = stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale
   805	    val width = bounds.w * stageScale
   806	    val height = bounds.h * stageScale
   807	
   808	    if (clipCircle) {
   809	        val clip = Path().apply {
   810	            addOval(Rect(left, top, left + width, top + height))
   811	        }
   812	        clipPath(clip) {
   813	            drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
   814	        }
   815	    } else {
   816	        drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
   817	    }
   818	}
   819	
   820	private fun DrawScope.drawImageBitmap(
   821	    image: ImageBitmap,
   822	    left: Float,
   823	    top: Float,
   824	    width: Float,
   825	    height: Float,
   826	    alpha: Float
   827	) {
   828	    drawImage(
   829	        image = image,
   830	        dstOffset = IntOffset(left.roundToInt(), top.roundToInt()),
   831	        dstSize = IntSize(width.roundToInt(), height.roundToInt()),
   832	        alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   833	    )
   834	}

   836	@Composable
   837	private fun SettlingLogoLayer(
   838	    scale: Float,
   839	    entryProgress: Float,
   840	    finalProgress: Float,
   841	    alpha: Float
   842	) {
   843	    val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
   844	    val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
   845	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   846	        end = baseBounds,
   847	        amount = entryProgress
   848	    )
   849	    val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
   850	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   851	        end = baseBounds,
   852	        amount = entryProgress
   853	    )
   854	    val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
   855	    val simpleBounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
   856	    val eyesBounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
   857	
   858	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
   859	        base = baseBounds,
   860	        target = simpleBounds,
   861	        scale = scale,
   862	        resId = R.drawable.logo_simple_large,
   863	        alpha = alpha
   864	    )
   865	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
   866	        base = baseBounds,
   867	        target = eyesBounds,
   868	        scale = scale,
   869	        resId = R.drawable.logo_eyes_large,
   870	        alpha = alpha
   871	    )
   872	}

   874	@Composable
   875	private fun AvatarSelectionLayer(scale: Float, alpha: Float, yOffset: Float) {
   876	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
   877	    val configuration = LocalConfiguration.current
   878	    val isLandscape = configuration.screenWidthDp > configuration.screenHeightDp
   879	    val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(isLandscape)
   880	    val accountPromptExtraShiftY = signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape)
   881	    val accountPromptTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape)
   882	    val accountPromptSecondaryTextSize = accountPromptTextSize * ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SECONDARY_TEXT_SCALE
   883	    Box(
   884	        modifier = Modifier
   885	            .requiredSize(figma(1194f, scale), figma(834f, scale))
   886	            .graphicsLayer {
   887	                this.alpha = alpha
   888	                translationY = yOffset * scale * density
   889	            }
   890	    ) {
   891	        // Figma Logo Slide 2, Property 1=Variant2, constrained inside Sign-in Frame.
   892	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 152f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   893	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 390f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   894	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 628f, y = 454f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   895	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 866f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   896	
   897	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 130f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.steve_name)
   898	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 368f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.martin_name)
   899	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 606f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_name)
   900	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 854f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 200f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_name)
   901	        SignInPromptText(
   902	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
   903	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LEFT_ANCHOR_X,
   904	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   905	            textSize = accountPromptSecondaryTextSize,
   906	            textAlign = TextAlign.Left,
   907	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   908	            scale = scale
   909	        )
   910	        SignInPromptText(
   911	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
   912	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X - ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH / 2f,
   913	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   914	            textSize = accountPromptTextSize,
   915	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   916	            scale = scale
   917	        )
   918	        SignInPromptText(
   919	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
   920	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_RIGHT_ANCHOR_X - ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH,
   921	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   922	            textSize = accountPromptSecondaryTextSize,
   923	            textAlign = TextAlign.Right,
   924	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   925	            scale = scale
   926	        )
   927	    }
   928	}
   929	
   930	@Composable
   931	private fun SignInPromptText(
   932	    text: String,
   933	    x: Float,
   934	    y: Float,
   935	    textSize: Float,
   936	    fontWeight: FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
   937	    textAlign: TextAlign = TextAlign.Center,
   938	    scale: Float
   939	) {
   940	    val contentAlignment = when (textAlign) {
   941	        TextAlign.Left -> Alignment.CenterStart
   942	        TextAlign.Right -> Alignment.CenterEnd
   943	        else -> Alignment.Center
   944	    }
   945	    Box(
   946	        modifier = Modifier
   947	            .absoluteOffset(
   948	                x = figma(x, scale),
   949	                y = figma(y, scale)
   950	            )
   951	            .requiredSize(
   952	                width = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH, scale),
   953	                height = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_HEIGHT, scale)
   954	            ),
   955	        contentAlignment = contentAlignment
   956	    ) {
   957	        Text(
   958	            text = text,
   959	            color = Color.White,
   960	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   961	            fontSize = (textSize * scale).sp,
   962	            fontWeight = fontWeight,
   963	            lineHeight = (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LINE_HEIGHT * textSize / ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * scale).sp,
   964	            maxLines = 1,
   965	            softWrap = false,
   966	            textAlign = textAlign
   967	        )
   968	    }
   969	}
   970	
   971	@Composable
   972	private fun FigmaAvatarImage(
   973	    x: Float,
   974	    y: Float,
   975	    w: Float,
   976	    h: Float,
   977	    scale: Float,
   978	    @DrawableRes resId: Int
   979	) {
   980	    Image(
   981	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
   982	        contentDescription = null,
   983	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
   984	        modifier = Modifier
   985	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
   986	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
   987	            .clip(CircleShape)
   988	    )
   989	}
   990	
   991	@Composable
   992	private fun LogoDot(x: Float, y: Float, scale: Float, active: Boolean) {
   993	    Box(
   994	        modifier = Modifier
   995	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
   996	            .requiredSize(figma(50.4f, scale), figma(50.4f, scale))
   997	            .clip(CircleShape)
   998	            .background(if (active) Color(0xFFE7E7E7) else Color(0xFF050505))
   999	    )
  1000	}
  1001	
  1002	@Composable
  1003	private fun PromoBackgroundLayer(
  1004	    scale: Float,
  1005	    darkOverlay: Float,
  1006	    @DrawableRes backgroundResId: Int,
  1007	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1008	) {
  1009	    // Nodes 793:7049 / 793:7050 at x:0 y:0 size:1584x1584.
  1010	    FigmaAssetImage(
  1011	        x = 0f,
  1012	        y = 0f,
  1013	        w = 1584f,
  1014	        h = 1584f,
  1015	        scale = scale,
  1016	        resId = backgroundResId,
  1017	        alpha = alpha
  1018	    )
  1019	
  1020	    if (darkOverlay > 0f) {
  1021	        Box(
  1022	            modifier = Modifier
  1023	                .absoluteOffset(x = figma(0f, scale), y = figma(0f, scale))
  1024	                .requiredSize(figma(1584f, scale), figma(1584f, scale))
  1025	                .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1026	                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = darkOverlay))
  1027	        )
  1028	    }
  1029	}
  1030	
  1031	@Composable
  1032	private fun TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1033	    base: FigmaBounds,
  1034	    target: FigmaBounds,
  1035	    scale: Float,
  1036	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1037	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1038	) {
  1039	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
  1040	    Image(
  1041	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1042	        contentDescription = null,
  1043	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1044	        modifier = Modifier
  1045	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(base.x, scale), y = figma(base.y, scale))
  1046	            .requiredSize(figma(base.w, scale), figma(base.h, scale))
  1047	            .graphicsLayer {
  1048	                this.alpha = alpha
  1049	                transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0f, 0f)
  1050	                translationX = (target.x - base.x) * scale * density
  1051	                translationY = (target.y - base.y) * scale * density
  1052	                scaleX = target.w / base.w
  1053	                scaleY = target.h / base.h
  1054	            }
  1055	    )
  1056	}
  1057	
  1058	@Composable
  1059	private fun FigmaAssetImage(
  1060	    x: Float,
  1061	    y: Float,
  1062	    w: Float,
  1063	    h: Float,
  1064	    scale: Float,
  1065	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1066	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1067	) {
  1068	    Image(
  1069	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1070	        contentDescription = null,
  1071	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1072	        modifier = Modifier
  1073	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
  1074	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
  1075	            .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1076	    )
  1077	}
  1078	
  1079	@Composable
  1080	private fun FigmaStage(background: Brush, content: @Composable BoxWithConstraintsScope.(Float) -> Unit) {
  1081	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1082	        val outerMaxWidth = this.maxWidth
  1083	        val outerMaxHeight = this.maxHeight
  1084	        val scale = min(outerMaxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, outerMaxHeight.value / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1085	        val stageWidth = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, scale)
  1086	        val stageHeight = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT, scale)
  1087	
  1088	        Box(
  1089	            modifier = Modifier
  1090	                .fillMaxSize()
  1091	                .background(background)
  1092	        ) {
  1093	            BoxWithConstraints(
  1094	                modifier = Modifier
  1095	                    .align(Alignment.Center)
  1096	                    .requiredSize(stageWidth, stageHeight)
  1097	            ) {
  1098	                content(scale)
  1099	            }
  1100	        }
  1101	    }
  1102	}
  1103	
  1104	private fun figma(px: Float, scale: Float) = (px * scale).dp
  1105	
  1106	private fun signInStackShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1107	    return if (isLandscape) {
  1108	        SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1109	    } else {
  1110	        SIGN_IN_PORTRAIT_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1111	    }
  1112	}
  1113	
  1114	private fun signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1115	    return if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1116	}
  1117	
  1118	private fun signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1119	    return if (isLandscape) ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LANDSCAPE_TEXT_SIZE else ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE
  1120	}
  1121	
  1122	private fun introBackgroundBrush(solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float): Brush {
  1123	    val solid = lerpColor(ColorFrame1Background, ColorFrame2Background, solidProgress)
  1124	    return Brush.verticalGradient(
  1125	        colors = listOf(
  1126	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientTop, gradientProgress),
  1127	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientCenter, gradientProgress),
  1128	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientBottom, gradientProgress)
  1129	        )
  1130	    )
  1131	}
  1132	
  1133	private fun solidBrush(color: Color): Brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
  1134	    colors = listOf(color, color)
  1135	)
  1136	
  1137	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  1138	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  1139	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1140	}
  1141	
  1142	private fun easedSegment(value: Float, start: Float, end: Float): Float {
  1143	    val progress = ((value - start) / (end - start)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1144	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(progress)
  1145	}
  1146	
  1147	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1148	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  1149	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1150	}
  1151	
  1152	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1153	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1154	}
  1155	
  1156	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1157	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1158	}
  1159	
  1160	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  1161	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1162	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  1163	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  1164	    return base + bounce
  1165	}
  1166	
  1167	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  1168	
  1169	private fun lerpBounds(start: FigmaBounds, end: FigmaBounds, amount: Float) = FigmaBounds(
  1170	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  1171	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  1172	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  1173	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  1174	)
  1175	
  1176	private val CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver = listSaver<CinerificIntroSnapshot, Any>(
  1177	    save = { snapshot ->
  1178	        listOf(
  1179	            snapshot.activeFlowName,
  1180	            snapshot.usernameText,
  1181	            snapshot.passwordText,
  1182	            snapshot.confirmPasswordText,
  1183	            snapshot.emailText,
  1184	            snapshot.monthText,
  1185	            snapshot.dayText,
  1186	            snapshot.yearText,
  1187	            snapshot.subscriptionTierText,
  1188	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName,
  1189	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName,
  1190	            snapshot.rememberMeChecked,
  1191	            snapshot.createAvatarIndex,
  1192	            snapshot.portraitProfileIndex
  1193	        )
  1194	    },
  1195	    restore = { values ->
  1196	        CinerificIntroSnapshot(
  1197	            activeFlowName = values.getOrNull(0) as? String ?: "",
  1198	            usernameText = values.getOrNull(1) as? String ?: "",
  1199	            passwordText = values.getOrNull(2) as? String ?: "",
  1200	            confirmPasswordText = values.getOrNull(3) as? String ?: "",
  1201	            emailText = values.getOrNull(4) as? String ?: "",
  1202	            monthText = values.getOrNull(5) as? String ?: "Mon",
  1203	            dayText = values.getOrNull(6) as? String ?: "Day",
  1204	            yearText = values.getOrNull(7) as? String ?: "Year",
  1205	            subscriptionTierText = values.getOrNull(8) as? String ?: "Subscription Tier",
  1206	            forgotRecoveryTargetName = values.getOrNull(9) as? String ?: "Password",
  1207	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName = values.getOrNull(10) as? String ?: "Idle",
  1208	            rememberMeChecked = values.getOrNull(11) as? Boolean ?: false,
  1209	            createAvatarIndex = values.getOrNull(12) as? Int ?: 0,
  1210	            portraitProfileIndex = values.getOrNull(13) as? Int ?: 0
  1211	        )
  1212	    }
  1213	)
