   429	    fun restoreIntroSnapshot(snapshot: CinerificIntroSnapshot) {
   430	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
   431	
   432	        val restoredFlow = enumValueOrNull<MockAccountFlow>(snapshot.activeFlowName)
   433	        val restoredRecoveryTarget = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotRecoveryTarget>(
   434	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName
   435	        ) ?: MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
   436	        val restoredSubmissionState = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState>(
   437	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName
   438	        ) ?: MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
   439	
   440	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   441	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   442	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   443	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   444	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   445	        pressedMockField = null
   446	        pressedRememberMe = false
   447	        pressedBackChevron = false
   448	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   449	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   450	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   451	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   452	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   453	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   454	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   455	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   456	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   457	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   458	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   459	        activeComposingText = ""
   460	        expandedMockDropdown = null
   461	        focusedMockField = null
   462	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
   463	        mockInputDimActiveField = null
   464	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
   465	
   466	        activeMockFlow = restoredFlow
   467	        if (restoredFlow == null) {
   468	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
   469	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
   470	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
   471	        } else {
   472	            mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   473	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
   474	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
   475	        }
   476	
   477	        rememberMeChecked = snapshot.rememberMeChecked
   478	        usernameText = snapshot.usernameText
   479	        passwordText = snapshot.passwordText
   480	        confirmPasswordText = snapshot.confirmPasswordText
   481	        emailText = snapshot.emailText
   482	        monthText = snapshot.monthText
   483	        dayText = snapshot.dayText
   484	        yearText = snapshot.yearText
   485	        subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(snapshot.subscriptionTierText)
   486	        forgotRecoveryTarget = restoredRecoveryTarget
   487	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = restoredSubmissionState
   488	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = if (
   489	            restoredSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
   490	        ) {
   491	            SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   492	        } else {
   493	            null
   494	        }
   495	
   496	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = snapshot.createAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
   497	        portraitProfileIndex = snapshot.portraitProfileIndex.coerceIn(0, FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.lastIndex)
   498	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = null
   499	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   500	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   501	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
   502	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = 0f
   503	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets.keys.forEach { dropdown ->
   504	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
   505	        }
   506	
   507	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   508	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   509	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   510	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   511	        appliedIntroSnapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
   512	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   513	    }
   514	
   515	    override fun onSizeChanged(width: Int, height: Int, oldWidth: Int, oldHeight: Int) {
   516	        super.onSizeChanged(width, height, oldWidth, oldHeight)
   517	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   518	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   519	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   520	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   521	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   522	        pressedMockField = null
   523	        pressedRememberMe = false
   524	        pressedBackChevron = false
   525	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   526	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   527	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   528	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   529	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   530	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   531	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   532	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   533	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   534	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   535	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   536	    }
   537	
   538	    override fun onApplyWindowInsets(insets: WindowInsets): WindowInsets {
   539	        val systemBars = WindowInsetsCompat.toWindowInsetsCompat(insets, this)
   540	            .getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
   541	        systemBarInsetTopPx = systemBars.top
   542	        systemBarInsetBottomPx = systemBars.bottom
   543	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   544	        return super.onApplyWindowInsets(insets)
   545	    }
   546	
   547	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   548	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   549	
   550	        val progress = bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis)
   551	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   552	            progress,
   553	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   554	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   555	        )
   556	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   557	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   558	
   559	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   560	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   561	        finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded()
   562	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   563	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   564	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   565	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   566	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   567	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   568	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   569	
   570	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   571	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   572	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha, formFocusMotion)
   573	        }
   574	
   575	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   576	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   577	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   578	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   579	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   580	            val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
   581	            if (stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   582	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
   583	                    drawPortraitProfile(canvas, stage, y, stackAlpha)
   584	                } else {
   585	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   586	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   587	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   588	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   589	
   590	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   591	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   592	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   593	                    drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   594	                }
   595	            }
   596	        }
   597	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   598	            val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
   599	            if (outgoingFlow != null) {
   600	                val switchMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
   601	                drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   602	                    canvas,
   603	                    outgoingFlow,
   604	                    stage.left,
   605	                    stage.top,
   606	                    stage.scale,
   607	                    1f - switchMotion,
   608	                    formFocusMotion,
   609	                    updateLabelAnimation = false
   610	                )
   611	                activeMockFlow?.let { incomingFlow ->
   612	                    drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   613	                        canvas,
   614	                        incomingFlow,
   615	                        stage.left,
   616	                        stage.top,
   617	                        stage.scale,
   618	                        switchMotion,
   619	                        formFocusMotion,
   620	                        updateLabelAnimation = true
   621	                    )
   622	                }
   623	            } else {
   624	                drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   625	            }
   626	            drawBackChevron(
   627	                canvas,
   628	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
   629	                mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
   630	                stage.left,
   631	                accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   632	                stage.scale,
   633	                mockMotion
   634	            )
   635	        }
   636	        drawAccountPrompt(
   637	            canvas = canvas,
   638	            stageLeft = stage.left,
   639	            stageTop = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) stage.top else accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   640	            stageScale = stage.scale,
   641	            yOffset = if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) {
   642	                lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha)
   643	            } else {
   644	                0f
   645	            },
   646	            alpha = avatarAlpha
   647	        )
   648	
   649	        if (
   650	            progress < 1f ||
   651	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   652	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating() ||
   653	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   654	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   655	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   656	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   657	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   658	            isMockInputDimAnimating()
   659	        ) {
   660	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   661	        }
   662	    }
   663	
   664	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   665	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   666	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   667	                mockTouchDownX = event.x
   668	                mockTouchDownY = event.y
   669	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
   670	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   671	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   672	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   673	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   674	                        return true
   675	                    }
   676	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   677	                    pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   678	                        settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   679	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   680	                        !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   681	                        settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   682	                    if (
   683	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   684	                        pressedSignInPrompt ||
   685	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   686	                    ) {
   687	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   688	                        return true
   689	                    }
   690	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   691	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   692	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   693	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   694	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   695	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   696	                    } else {
   697	                        null
   698	                    }
   699	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   700	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   701	                    } else {
   702	                        null
   703	                    }
   704	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   705	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   706	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   707	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   708	                    ) {
   709	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   710	                    } else {
   711	                        null
   712	                    }
   713	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   714	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   715	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   716	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   717	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   718	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   719	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   720	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   721	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   722	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   723	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   724	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   725	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   726	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   727	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   728	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   729	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   730	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   731	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   732	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   733	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   734	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   735	                    ) {
   736	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   737	                    } else {
   738	                        null
   739	                    }
   740	                    pressedMockField = if (
   741	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   742	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   743	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   744	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   745	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   746	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   747	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   748	                    ) {
   749	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   750	                    } else {
   751	                        null
   752	                    }
   753	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   754	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   755	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   756	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   757	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   758	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   759	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   760	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   761	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   762	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   763	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   764	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   765	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   766	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   767	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   768	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   769	                    return true
   770	                }
   771	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   772	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   773	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   774	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   775	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   776	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   777	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   778	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   779	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   780	                ) {
   781	                    null
   782	                } else {
   783	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   784	                }
   785	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   786	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   787	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   788	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   789	            }
   790	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   791	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   792	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   793	                        return true
   794	                    }
   795	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   796	                        return true
   797	                    }
   798	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   799	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   800	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   801	                    }
   802	                    return true
   803	                }
   804	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout() && pressedAvatarProfile != null) {
   805	                    val horizontalDelta = event.x - mockTouchDownX
   806	                    val verticalDelta = event.y - mockTouchDownY
   807	                    if (abs(horizontalDelta) > touchSlop && abs(horizontalDelta) > abs(verticalDelta)) {
   808	                        portraitProfileSwipeMoved = true
   809	                    }
   810	                    return true
   811	                }
   812	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   813	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   814	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   815	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null
   816	            }
   817	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   818	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
   819	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   820	                    pressedMockField = null
   821	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   822	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   823	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   824	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   825	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   826	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   827	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   828	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   829	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   830	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   831	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   832	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   833	                    return true
   834	                }
   835	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   836	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   837	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   838	                        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   839	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   840	                        return true
   841	                    }
   842	                    val requestedFlow = when {
   843	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   844	                            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
   845	                        }
   846	                        pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   847	                            MockAccountFlow.SignIn
   848	                        }
   849	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt && settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
   850	                            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
   851	                        }
   852	                        else -> null
   853	                    }
   854	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   855	                    pressedSignInPrompt = false
   856	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   857	                    if (requestedFlow != null) {
   858	                        super.performClick()
   859	                        switchMockAccountFlow(requestedFlow)
   860	                        return true
   861	                    }
   862	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   863	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   864	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   865	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   866	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   867	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   868	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   869	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   870	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
   871	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
   872	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
   873	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
   874	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
   875	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
   876	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
   877	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
   878	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
   879	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
   880	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
   881	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
   882	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
   883	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
   884	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
   885	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
   886	                        pressedOption != null &&
   887	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
   888	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
   889	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
   890	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
   891	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
   892	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
   893	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
   894	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
   895	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
   896	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
   897	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   898	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
   899	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
   900	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
   901	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
   902	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
   903	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
   904	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
   905	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
   906	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   907	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
   908	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
   909	                        pressedOption == null &&
   910	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   911	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
   912	                    pressedMockField = null
   913	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   914	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   915	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   916	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   917	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   918	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   919	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   920	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   921	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   922	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   923	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   924	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   925	                    super.performClick()
   926	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
   927	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   928	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
   929	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   930	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   931	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
   932	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
   933	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   934	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   935	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
   936	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
   937	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   938	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
   939	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
   940	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   941	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   942	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   943	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   944	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   945	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
   946	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
   947	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
   948	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   949	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   950	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   951	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   952	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
   953	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
   954	                        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
   955	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   956	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
   957	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
   958	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
   959	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
   960	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
   961	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
   962	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   963	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   964	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
   965	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
   966	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   967	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
   968	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
   969	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
   970	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   971	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   972	                        }
   973	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   974	                    } else {
   975	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   976	                    }
   977	                    return true
   978	                }
   979	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   980	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   981	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   982	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
   983	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   984	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   985	                val swipeDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
   986	                val swipeThreshold = PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_COMMIT_DP * resources.displayMetrics.density
   987	                val shouldSwipeProfile = isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
   988	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null &&
   989	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
   990	                    abs(swipeDeltaX) >= swipeThreshold
   991	                val shouldNavigate = !shouldSwipeProfile && !portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
   992	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null && pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
   993	                clickAvatarProfile = pressedAvatarProfile
   994	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
   995	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   996	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
   997	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   998	                if (shouldSwipeProfile) {
   999	                    portraitProfileIndex = if (swipeDeltaX < 0f) {
  1000	                        (portraitProfileIndex + 1) % FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.size
  1001	                    } else {
  1002	                        (portraitProfileIndex - 1 + FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.size) % FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.size
  1003	                    }
  1004	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1005	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1006	                    notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  1007	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1008	                    true
  1009	                } else if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
  1010	                    super.performClick()
  1011	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
  1012	                    true
  1013	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
  1014	                    super.performClick()
  1015	                    openMockSignInScreen()
  1016	                    true
  1017	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
  1018	                    super.performClick()
  1019	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
  1020	                    true
  1021	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
  1022	                    performClick()
  1023	                    true
  1024	                } else {
  1025	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1026	                    false
  1027	                }
  1028	            }
  1029	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
  1030	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1031	                clickAvatarProfile = null
  1032	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1033	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1034	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1035	                pressedMockField = null
  1036	                pressedRememberMe = false
  1037	                pressedBackChevron = false
  1038	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1039	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1040	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1041	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1042	                pressedMockDropdown = null
  1043	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1044	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1045	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1046	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1047	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1048	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  1049	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1050	                false
  1051	            }
  1052	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
  1053	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
  1054	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
  1055	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
  1056	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
  1057	        }
  1058	    }
  1059	
  1060	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
  1061	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
  1062	    }
  1063	
  1064	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
  1065	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
  1066	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
  1067	            MockSignInField.Password,
  1068	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
  1069	            MockSignInField.Email -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
  1070	            MockSignInField.Month,
  1071	            MockSignInField.Day,
  1072	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
  1073	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
  1074	        }
  1075	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
  1076	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
  1077	        } else {
  1078	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
  1079	        }
  1080	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
  1081	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
  1082	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
  1083	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
  1084	    }
  1085	
  1086	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1087	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
  1088	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1089	        }
  1090	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1091	    }
  1092	
  1093	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
  1094	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1095	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
  1096	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
  1097	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
  1098	        }
  1099	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
  1100	    }
  1101	
  1102	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
  1103	        super.performClick()
  1104	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
  1105	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
  1106	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
  1107	        clickAvatarProfile = null
  1108	        return true
  1109	    }
  1110	
  1111	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
  1112	        canvas: Canvas,
  1113	        stageLeft: Float,
  1114	        stageTop: Float,
  1115	        stageScale: Float,
  1116	        progress: Float,
  1117	        alpha: Float,
  1118	        formFocusMotion: Float
  1119	    ) {
  1120	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1121	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1122	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1123	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1124	        val portraitLogo = Bounds(
  1125	            (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - 600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE) / 2f +
  1126	                PORTRAIT_LOGO_VISIBLE_CENTER_OFFSET_X,
  1127	            portraitLogoTop(),
  1128	            600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE,
  1129	            LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  1130	        )
  1131	        val base = offsetBounds(
  1132	            lerpBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), portraitLogo, portraitAmount),
  1133	            focusShiftY
  1134	        )
  1135	        val final = offsetBounds(
  1136	            lerpBounds(Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT), portraitLogo, portraitAmount),
  1137	            focusShiftY
  1138	        )
  1139	
  1140	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
  1141	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1142	            return
  1143	        }
  1144	
  1145	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1146	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1147	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1148	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1149	    }

  1151	    private fun drawPortraitProfile(
  1152	        canvas: Canvas,
  1153	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1154	        yOffset: Float,
  1155	        alpha: Float
  1156	    ) {
  1157	        val profile = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  1158	        val avatarBitmap = when (profile) {
  1159	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveAvatar
  1160	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinAvatar
  1161	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyAvatar
  1162	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestAvatar
  1163	        }
  1164	        val nameBitmap = when (profile) {
  1165	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveName
  1166	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinName
  1167	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyName
  1168	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestName
  1169	        }
  1170	        val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  1171	        val nameWidth = (if (profile == CinerificProfile.Guest) 200f else 220f) * PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  1172	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1173	            canvas,
  1174	            avatarBitmap,
  1175	            Bounds(
  1176	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - avatarSize) / 2f,
  1177	                portraitAvatarTop() + yOffset,
  1178	                avatarSize,
  1179	                avatarSize
  1180	            ),
  1181	            stage.left,
  1182	            stage.top,
  1183	            stage.scale,
  1184	            alpha
  1185	        )
  1186	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1187	            canvas,
  1188	            nameBitmap,
  1189	            Bounds(
  1190	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - nameWidth) / 2f,
  1191	                portraitProfileNameTop() + yOffset,
  1192	                nameWidth,
  1193	                portraitProfileNameHeight()
  1194	            ),
  1195	            stage.left,
  1196	            stage.top,
  1197	            stage.scale,
  1198	            alpha
  1199	        )
  1200	    }
  1201	
  1202	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
  1203	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
  1204	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
  1205	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
  1206	            return
  1207	        }
  1208	
  1209	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
  1210	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1211	            0f,
  1212	            0f,
  1213	            0f,
  1214	            height.toFloat(),
  1215	            intArrayOf(
  1216	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
  1217	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
  1218	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
  1219	            ),
  1220	            null,
  1221	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1222	        )
  1223	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1224	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
  1225	
  1226	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
  1227	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
  1228	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
  1229	        }
  1230	    }
  1231	
  1232	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
  1233	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
  1234	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1235	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1236	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
  1237	    }
  1238	
  1239	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
  1240	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
  1241	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1242	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1243	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1244	    }
  1245	
  1246	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
  1247	        canvas: Canvas,
  1248	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  1249	        bounds: Bounds,
  1250	        stageLeft: Float,
  1251	        stageTop: Float,
  1252	        stageScale: Float,
  1253	        alpha: Float
  1254	    ) {
  1255	        tempRect.set(
  1256	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
  1257	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
  1258	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
  1259	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
  1260	        )
  1261	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1262	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1263	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1264	    }
  1265	
  1266	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
  1267	        canvas: Canvas,
  1268	        stageLeft: Float,
  1269	        stageTop: Float,
  1270	        stageScale: Float,
  1271	        yOffset: Float,
  1272	        alpha: Float
  1273	    ) {
  1274	        val isLandscape = width > height
  1275	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1276	        val textSize = lerpFloat(
  1277	            signInAccountPromptTextSize(),
  1278	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE,
  1279	            portraitAmount
  1280	        ) * stageScale
  1281	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1282	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
  1283	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
  1284	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1285	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1286	
  1287	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1288	        val standardCenterY = stageTop +
  1289	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
  1290	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
  1291	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1292	        fun promptCenterY(portraitCenterY: Float): Float = lerpFloat(
  1293	            standardCenterY,
  1294	            stageTop + (portraitCenterY + yOffset) * stageScale,
  1295	            portraitAmount
  1296	        )
  1297	        val createCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptCreateCenterY())
  1298	        val signInCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptSignInCenterY())
  1299	        val forgotCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptForgotCenterY())
  1300	        val createBaselineY = createCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1301	        val signInBaselineY = signInCenterY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1302	        val forgotBaselineY = forgotCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1303	        val portraitCenterX = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH / 2f
  1304	        if (
  1305	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  1306	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  1307	        ) {
  1308	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1309	            canvas.drawText(
  1310	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1311	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptCreateCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1312	                createBaselineY,
  1313	                accountPromptPaint
  1314	            )
  1315	        }
  1316	        if (
  1317	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn &&
  1318	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  1319	        ) {
  1320	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1321	            canvas.drawText(
  1322	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1323	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X, portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1324	                signInBaselineY,
  1325	                formTitlePaint
  1326	            )
  1327	        }
  1328	        if (
  1329	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  1330	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  1331	        ) {
  1332	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1333	            canvas.drawText(
  1334	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1335	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptForgotCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1336	                forgotBaselineY,
  1337	                accountPromptPaint
  1338	            )
  1339	        }
  1340	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
  1341	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1342	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1343	    }

  1345	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
  1346	        canvas: Canvas,
  1347	        stageLeft: Float,
  1348	        stageTop: Float,
  1349	        stageScale: Float,
  1350	        progress: Float,
  1351	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1352	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean = true
  1353	    ) {
  1354	        if (updateLabelAnimation) {
  1355	            updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1356	        }
  1357	
  1358	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1359	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1360	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1361	            return
  1362	        }
  1363	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1364	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1365	            return
  1366	        }
  1367	
  1368	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1369	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1370	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1371	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
  1372	        val usernameY = MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset
  1373	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_GAP
  1374	
  1375	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1376	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1377	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1378	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1379	
  1380	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1381	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1382	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1383	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1384	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1385	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1386	            canvas,
  1387	            fieldX,
  1388	            rememberMeY,
  1389	            stageLeft,
  1390	            stageTop,
  1391	            stageScale,
  1392	            alpha
  1393	        )
  1394	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1395	            canvas,
  1396	            stageLeft,
  1397	            stageTop,
  1398	            stageScale,
  1399	            alpha,
  1400	            yOffset,
  1401	            focusShiftY
  1402	        )
  1403	
  1404	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1405	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1406	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1407	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1408	    }

  1590	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1591	        canvas: Canvas,
  1592	        stageLeft: Float,
  1593	        stageTop: Float,
  1594	        stageScale: Float,
  1595	        alpha: Float,
  1596	        yOffset: Float,
  1597	        focusShiftY: Float
  1598	    ) {
  1599	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1600	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  1601	
  1602	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1603	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  1604	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  1605	
  1606	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1607	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1608	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1609	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  1610	
  1611	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  1612	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1613	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  1614	                canvas,
  1615	                activeDropdown,
  1616	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  1617	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1618	                stageLeft,
  1619	                stageTop,
  1620	                stageScale,
  1621	                alpha,
  1622	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  1623	            )
  1624	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  1625	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1626	            }
  1627	            return
  1628	        }
  1629	
  1630	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  1631	        drawMockSignInField(
  1632	            canvas,
  1633	            activeField,
  1634	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  1635	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1636	            stageLeft,
  1637	            stageTop,
  1638	            stageScale,
  1639	            alpha,
  1640	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  1641	        )
  1642	    }

  1644	    private fun drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
  1645	        canvas: Canvas,
  1646	        flow: MockAccountFlow,
  1647	        stageLeft: Float,
  1648	        stageTop: Float,
  1649	        stageScale: Float,
  1650	        progress: Float,
  1651	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1652	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean
  1653	    ) {
  1654	        val currentFlow = activeMockFlow
  1655	        activeMockFlow = flow
  1656	        try {
  1657	            drawMockSignInForm(
  1658	                canvas,
  1659	                stageLeft,
  1660	                stageTop,
  1661	                stageScale,
  1662	                progress,
  1663	                formFocusMotion,
  1664	                updateLabelAnimation
  1665	            )
  1666	        } finally {
  1667	            activeMockFlow = currentFlow
  1668	        }
  1669	    }

  1671	    private fun drawMockSignInTitle(
  1672	        canvas: Canvas,

  1675	        stageTop: Float,
  1676	        stageScale: Float,
  1677	        alpha: Float
  1678	    ) {

  1680	        val sourceTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  1681	        val targetTextSize = MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1682	        val sourceCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1683	        val targetCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  1684	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop +
  1685	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stageScale

  1690

  1697	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),

  1699	        )
  1700	    }

  1764	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  1765	        canvas: Canvas,
  1766	        x: Float,
  1767	        y: Float,
  1768	        stageLeft: Float,
  1769	        stageTop: Float,
  1770	        stageScale: Float,
  1771	        alpha: Float,
  1772	        buttonWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  1773	    ) {
  1774	        tempRect.set(
  1775	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  1776	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  1777	            stageLeft + (x + buttonWidth) * stageScale,
  1778	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  1779	        )
  1780	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  1781	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  1782	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  1783	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  1784	        val fillAlpha = when {
  1785	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  1786	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  1787	            pressed -> 92f
  1788	            enabled -> 68f
  1789	            else -> 18f
  1790	        }
  1791	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  1792	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  1793	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  1794	            pressed -> 238f
  1795	            enabled -> 210f
  1796	            else -> 118f
  1797	        }
  1798	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1799	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1800	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  1801	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1802	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1803	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1804	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  1805	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  1806	        }
  1807	
  1808	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1809	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1810	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1811	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  1812	        val maxTextWidth = buttonWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  1813	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  1814	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  1815	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  1816	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  1817	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  1818	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  1819	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  1820	            formTitlePaint
  1821	        )
  1822	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  1823	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  1824	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  1825	            enabled -> 255f
  1826	            else -> 138f
  1827	        }).roundToInt()
  1828	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1829	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  1830	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  1831	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  1832	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  1833	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  1834	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  1835	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  1836	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  1837	            canvas.drawLine(
  1838	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  1839	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  1840	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1841	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1842	                backChevronPaint
  1843	            )
  1844	            canvas.drawLine(
  1845	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  1846	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  1847	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  1848	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  1849	                backChevronPaint
  1850	            )
  1851	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  1852	        } else {
  1853	            tempRect.centerX()
  1854	        }
  1855	        canvas.drawText(
  1856	            buttonText,
  1857	            textX,
  1858	            baselineY,
  1859	            formTitlePaint
  1860	        )
  1861	    }
  1862	
  1863	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  1864	        canvas: Canvas,
  1865	        buttonRect: RectF,
  1866	        radius: Float,
  1867	        alpha: Float
  1868	    ) {
  1869	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  1870	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  1871	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  1872	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  1873	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  1874	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1875	            sweepLeft,
  1876	            buttonRect.top,
  1877	            sweepRight,
  1878	            buttonRect.top,
  1879	            intArrayOf(
  1880	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  1881	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  1882	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  1883	            ),
  1884	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  1885	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1886	        )
  1887	        canvas.save()
  1888	        tempPath.reset()
  1889	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  1890	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  1891	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  1892	        canvas.restore()
  1893	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  1894	    }

  1896	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoverySelector(
  1897	        canvas: Canvas,

  1922	        )

  1938	            layout.boxWidth,

  1940	            alpha
  1941	        )

  1943	            canvas,
  1944	            MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password,
  1945	            layout.passwordBoxX,
  1946	            rowTop,
  1947	            layout.boxWidth,
  1948	            stageScale,
  1949	            alpha

  1953	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(
  1954	        canvas: Canvas,
  1955	        target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget,
  1956	        left: Float,
  1957	        top: Float,
  1958	        boxWidth: Float,
  1959	        stageScale: Float,
  1960	        alpha: Float
  1961	    ) {
  1962	        val right = left + boxWidth
  1963	        val bottom = top + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_HEIGHT * stageScale
  1964	        tempRect.set(left, top, right, bottom)
  1965	        val selected = forgotRecoveryTarget == target
  1966	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target
  1967	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * when {
  1968	            pressed -> 82f
  1969	            selected -> 64f
  1970	            else -> 14f
  1971	        }).roundToInt()
  1972	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * when {
  1973	            pressed -> 245f
  1974	            selected -> 235f
  1975	            else -> 142f
  1976	        }).roundToInt()
  1977	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  1978	
  1979	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1980	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1981	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  1982	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  1983	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  1984	
  1985	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  1986	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (selected || pressed) 255f else 178f).roundToInt()
  1987	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1988	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  1989	        canvas.drawText(
  1990	            target.displayText,
  1991	            tempRect.centerX(),
  1992	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  1993	            formInputPaint
  1994	        )
  1995	    }

  1997	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  1998	        canvas: Canvas,
  1999	        text: String,
  2000	        centerX: Float,
  2001	        topY: Float,
  2002	        maxWidth: Float,
  2003	        stageLeft: Float,
  2004	        stageTop: Float,
  2005	        stageScale: Float,
  2006	        alpha: Float
  2007	    ): Float {
  2008	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  2009	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2010	            canvas,
  2011	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  2012	            centerX,
  2013	            topY,
  2014	            maxWidth,
  2015	            stageLeft,
  2016	            stageTop,
  2017	            stageScale,
  2018	            alpha,
  2019	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  2020	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT
  2021	        )
  2022	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  2023	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2024	            canvas,
  2025	            middleText,
  2026	            centerX,
  2027	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2028	            maxWidth,
  2029	            stageLeft,
  2030	            stageTop,
  2031	            stageScale,
  2032	            alpha,
  2033	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2034	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2035	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2036	        )
  2037	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  2038	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  2039	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  2040	            canvas,
  2041	            bottomText,
  2042	            centerX,
  2043	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2044	            maxWidth,
  2045	            stageLeft,
  2046	            stageTop,
  2047	            stageScale,
  2048	            alpha,
  2049	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2050	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2051	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2052	        )
  2053	    }
  2054	
  2055	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  2056	        canvas: Canvas,
  2057	        text: String,
  2058	        centerX: Float,
  2059	        topY: Float,
  2060	        maxWidth: Float,
  2061	        stageLeft: Float,
  2062	        stageTop: Float,
  2063	        stageScale: Float,
  2064	        alpha: Float,
  2065	        textSize: Float,
  2066	        lineHeight: Float,
  2067	        textAlign: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2068	    ): Float {
  2069	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  2070	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  2071	        formInputPaint.textAlign = textAlign
  2072	
  2073	        val lines = wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2074	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2075	        val x = when (textAlign) {
  2076	            Paint.Align.LEFT -> stageLeft + (centerX - maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2077	            Paint.Align.RIGHT -> stageLeft + (centerX + maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2078	            Paint.Align.CENTER -> stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2079	        }
  2080	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2081	        lines.forEach { line ->
  2082	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  2083	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  2084	        }
  2085	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  2086	    }
  2087	
  2088	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2089	        canvas: Canvas,
  2090	        columnX: Float,
  2091	        firstFieldY: Float,
  2092	        stageLeft: Float,
  2093	        stageTop: Float,
  2094	        stageScale: Float,
  2095	        alpha: Float
  2096	    ) {
  2097	        val avatarScale = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  2098	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * avatarScale
  2099	        val labelBaselineFromTop = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_LABEL_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * avatarScale
  2100	        val avatarTopGap = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_TOP_GAP * avatarScale
  2101	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  2102	        val baselineY = stageTop + (firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop) * stageScale
  2103	        formLabelPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * avatarScale * stageScale
  2104	        formLabelPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2105	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2106	        canvas.drawText(
  2107	            MOCK_FORM_AVATAR_TEXT,
  2108	            centerX,
  2109	            baselineY,
  2110	            formLabelPaint
  2111	        )
  2112	
  2113	        val avatarLeftX = columnX + (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - scaledAvatarSize) / 2f
  2114	        val avatarTopY = firstFieldY + labelBaselineFromTop + avatarTopGap
  2115	        val avatarBounds = Bounds(
  2116	            avatarLeftX,
  2117	            avatarTopY,
  2118	            scaledAvatarSize,
  2119	            scaledAvatarSize
  2120	        )
  2121	        val carouselProgress = mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  2122	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  2123	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize)
  2124	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  2125	        val dragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  2126	
  2127	        drawMockCreateAvatarBubbleShell(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  2128	
  2129	        canvas.save()
  2130	        clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  2131	        if (
  2132	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  2133	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  2134	            dragDirection != null &&
  2135	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  2136	        ) {
  2137	            val dragSlideDirection = dragDirection.stageDirection
  2138	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2139	                canvas,
  2140	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  2141	                avatarBounds,
  2142	                -dragSlideDirection * dragProgress * slideDistance,
  2143	                stageLeft,
  2144	                stageTop,
  2145	                stageScale,
  2146	                alpha
  2147	            )
  2148	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2149	                canvas,
  2150	                mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  2151	                avatarBounds,
  2152	                dragSlideDirection * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance,
  2153	                stageLeft,
  2154	                stageTop,
  2155	                stageScale,
  2156	                alpha
  2157	            )
  2158	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  2159	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2160	                canvas,
  2161	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex,
  2162	                avatarBounds,
  2163	                -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance,
  2164	                stageLeft,
  2165	                stageTop,
  2166	                stageScale,
  2167	                alpha
  2168	            )
  2169	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2170	                canvas,
  2171	                mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex,
  2172	                avatarBounds,
  2173	                slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance,
  2174	                stageLeft,
  2175	                stageTop,
  2176	                stageScale,
  2177	                alpha
  2178	            )
  2179	        } else {
  2180	            drawMockCreateAvatarBitmap(
  2181	                canvas,
  2182	                mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  2183	                avatarBounds,
  2184	                0f,
  2185	                stageLeft,
  2186	                stageTop,
  2187	                stageScale,
  2188	                alpha
  2189	            )
  2190	        }
  2191	        canvas.restore()
  2192	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2193	            canvas,
  2194	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2195	            avatarBounds,
  2196	            stageLeft,
  2197	            stageTop,
  2198	            stageScale,
  2199	            alpha
  2200	        )
  2201	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2202	            canvas,
  2203	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2204	            avatarBounds,
  2205	            stageLeft,
  2206	            stageTop,
  2207	            stageScale,
  2208	            alpha
  2209	        )
  2210	    }

  2252	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarBubbleInterior(
  2253	        canvas: Canvas,
  2254	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2255	        stageLeft: Float,
  2256	        stageTop: Float,
  2257	        stageScale: Float
  2258	    ) {

  2260	        tempPath.addCircle(
  2261	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,
  2262	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  2263	            mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds, stageScale),
  2264	            Path.Direction.CW
  2265	        )

  2267	    }

  2269	    private fun mockCreateAvatarBubbleInnerRadius(avatarBounds: Bounds, stageScale: Float): Float {
  2270	        return (avatarBounds.w / 2f - avatarBounds.w * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO) * stageScale
  2271	    }

  2273	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2274	        canvas: Canvas,
  2275	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2276	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2277	        stageLeft: Float,
  2278	        stageTop: Float,
  2279	        stageScale: Float,
  2280	        alpha: Float
  2281	    ) {
  2282	        val centerX = stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2283	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  2284	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2285	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale / 2f
  2286	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  2287	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2288	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2289	
  2290	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2291	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2292	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2293	        } else {
  2294	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  2295	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  2296	        }
  2297	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2298	    }
