  2300	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  2301	        canvas: Canvas,
  2302	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2303	        x: Float,
  2304	        y: Float,
  2305	        stageLeft: Float,
  2306	        stageTop: Float,
  2307	        stageScale: Float,
  2308	        alpha: Float,
  2309	        dropdownWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2310	    ) {
  2311	        tempRect.set(
  2312	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2313	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2314	            stageLeft + (x + dropdownWidth) * stageScale,
  2315	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2316	        )
  2317	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2318	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2319	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2320	
  2321	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2322	        formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2323	        formInputPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2324	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2325	        val textInset = mockDropdownTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2326	        val chevronInset = mockDropdownChevronInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2327	        val displayText = mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown)
  2328	        val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(displayText)
  2329	            ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2330	        val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2331	            (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2332	        } else {
  2333	            0f
  2334	        }
  2335	        val maxTextWidth = (dropdownWidth * stageScale - textInset - iconLaneWidth - chevronInset * 2f)
  2336	            .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2337	        if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2338	            drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2339	                canvas = canvas,
  2340	                bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2341	                left = tempRect.left + textInset,
  2342	                centerY = tempRect.centerY(),
  2343	                iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_CONTROL_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2344	                alpha = textAlpha
  2345	            )
  2346	        }
  2347	        canvas.drawText(
  2348	            trailingFittingText(displayText, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2349	            tempRect.left + textInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2350	            tempRect.centerY() - (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  2351	            formInputPaint
  2352	        )
  2353	
  2354	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  2355	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  2356	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  2357	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2358	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale
  2359	        canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2360	        canvas.drawLine(centerX, centerY + halfSize / 2f, centerX + halfSize, centerY - halfSize / 2f, backChevronPaint)
  2361	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2362	    }

  2364	    private fun drawMockDropdownOptions(
  2365	        canvas: Canvas,
  2366	        stageLeft: Float,
  2367	        stageTop: Float,
  2368	        stageScale: Float,
  2369	        alpha: Float
  2370	    ) {
  2371	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return
  2372	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return
  2373	
  2374	        val x = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  2375	        val y = mockDropdownOptionsY(
  2376	            dropdown,
  2377	            MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha),
  2378	            mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  2379	        )
  2380	        val left = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  2381	        val top = stageTop + y * stageScale
  2382	        val right = stageLeft + (x + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stageScale
  2383	        val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stageScale
  2384	        val optionAlpha = 255
  2385	        val optionTextInset = mockDropdownOptionTextInsetX(dropdown) * stageScale
  2386	        val scrollbarReservedWidth = if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2387	            (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stageScale
  2388	        } else {
  2389	            0f
  2390	        }
  2391	
  2392	        val visibleOptions = mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown)
  2393	        visibleOptions.forEachIndexed { index, option ->
  2394	            val optionTop = top + index * optionHeight
  2395	            val optionBottom = optionTop + optionHeight
  2396	            val fillColor = if (option == mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)) {
  2397	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 76, 12, 94)
  2398	            } else {
  2399	                Color.argb(optionAlpha, 14, 2, 18)
  2400	            }
  2401	            tempRect.set(left, optionTop, right, optionBottom)
  2402	            formFieldFillPaint.color = fillColor
  2403	            formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(optionAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2404	            formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.2f * stageScale
  2405	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldFillPaint)
  2406	            canvas.drawRect(tempRect, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2407	
  2408	            formInputPaint.textSize = mockDropdownOptionTextSize(dropdown) * stageScale
  2409	            formInputPaint.alpha = optionAlpha
  2410	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2411	            val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2412	            val membershipIcon = membershipTierArtworkIconForOption(option)
  2413	                ?.takeIf { dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier }
  2414	            val iconLaneWidth = if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2415	                (MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE + MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_ICON_TEXT_GAP) * stageScale
  2416	            } else {
  2417	                0f
  2418	            }
  2419	            if (membershipIcon != null) {
  2420	                drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2421	                    canvas = canvas,
  2422	                    bitmap = membershipIcon,
  2423	                    left = left + optionTextInset,
  2424	                    centerY = (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f,
  2425	                    iconSize = MOCK_MEMBERSHIP_TIER_OPTION_ICON_SIZE * stageScale,
  2426	                    alpha = optionAlpha
  2427	                )
  2428	            }
  2429	            val maxTextWidth = (right - left - optionTextInset - iconLaneWidth - scrollbarReservedWidth)
  2430	                .coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2431	            canvas.drawText(
  2432	                trailingFittingText(option, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  2433	                left + optionTextInset + iconLaneWidth,
  2434	                (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2435	                formInputPaint
  2436	            )
  2437	        }
  2438	
  2439	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  2440	            drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2441	                canvas,
  2442	                dropdown,
  2443	                top,
  2444	                right,
  2445	                optionHeight * visibleOptions.size,
  2446	                stageScale,
  2447	                optionAlpha
  2448	            )
  2449	        }
  2450	    }
  2451	
  2452	    private fun drawMembershipTierIcon(
  2453	        canvas: Canvas,
  2454	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  2455	        left: Float,
  2456	        centerY: Float,
  2457	        iconSize: Float,
  2458	        alpha: Int
  2459	    ) {
  2460	        val bitmapAspect = bitmap.width.toFloat() / bitmap.height.toFloat()
  2461	        val width: Float
  2462	        val height: Float
  2463	        if (bitmapAspect >= 1f) {
  2464	            width = iconSize
  2465	            height = iconSize / bitmapAspect
  2466	        } else {
  2467	            width = iconSize * bitmapAspect
  2468	            height = iconSize
  2469	        }
  2470	        val top = centerY - height / 2f
  2471	        tempIconRect.set(left, top, left + width, top + height)
  2472	        imagePaint.alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0, 255)
  2473	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempIconRect, imagePaint)
  2474	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  2475	    }
  2476	
  2477	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  2478	        canvas: Canvas,
  2479	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  2480	        top: Float,
  2481	        right: Float,
  2482	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  2483	        stageScale: Float,
  2484	        alpha: Int
  2485	    ) {
  2486	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  2487	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  2488	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  2489	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  2490	
  2491	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  2492	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  2493	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2494	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  2495	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  2496	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  2497	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  2498	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  2499	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  2500	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2501	
  2502	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  2503	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2504	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2505	
  2506	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  2507	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2508	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2509	    }

  2511	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  2512	        canvas: Canvas,
  2513	        field: MockSignInField,
  2514	        x: Float,
  2515	        y: Float,
  2516	        stageLeft: Float,
  2517	        stageTop: Float,
  2518	        stageScale: Float,
  2519	        alpha: Float,
  2520	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2521	    ) {
  2522	        tempRect.set(
  2523	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2524	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2525	            stageLeft + (x + fieldWidth) * stageScale,
  2526	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2527	        )
  2528	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
  2529	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2530	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2531	
  2532	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  2533	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  2534	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2535	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  2536	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  2537	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  2538	            labelProgress
  2539	        ) * stageScale
  2540	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2541	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2542	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  2543	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2544	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2545	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  2546	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2547	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
  2548	            labelProgress
  2549	        )
  2550	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  2551	        canvas.drawText(
  2552	            label,
  2553	            labelX,
  2554	            labelBaselineY,
  2555	            formLabelPaint
  2556	        )
  2557	
  2558	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  2559	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  2560	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  2561	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  2562	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2563	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2564	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2565	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  2566	
  2567	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
  2568	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
  2569	            val maxInputWidth = fieldWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2570	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  2571	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  2572	
  2573	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  2574	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
  2575	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2576	                canvas.drawLine(
  2577	                    caretX,
  2578	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  2579	                    caretX,
  2580	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  2581	                    formCaretPaint
  2582	                )
  2583	            }
  2584	        }
  2585	    }

  2587	    private fun drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  2588	        canvas: Canvas,
  2589	        x: Float,
  2590	        y: Float,
  2591	        stageLeft: Float,
  2592	        stageTop: Float,
  2593	        stageScale: Float,
  2594	        alpha: Float
  2595	    ) {
  2596	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  2597	        val boxLeft = stageLeft + x * stageScale
  2598	        val boxTop = stageTop + y * stageScale
  2599	        val boxRight = boxLeft + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  2600	        val boxBottom = boxTop + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_SIZE * stageScale
  2601	        val fillAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked || pressedRememberMe) 46f else 18f).roundToInt()
  2602	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * if (rememberMeChecked) 220f else 168f).roundToInt()
  2603	
  2604	        rememberMeBoxFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2605	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2606	        rememberMeBoxStrokePaint.strokeWidth = 1.7f * stageScale
  2607	        tempRect.set(boxLeft, boxTop, boxRight, boxBottom)
  2608	        val radius = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_BOX_RADIUS * stageScale
  2609	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxFillPaint)
  2610	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, rememberMeBoxStrokePaint)
  2611	
  2612	        if (rememberMeChecked) {
  2613	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2614	            rememberMeCheckPaint.strokeWidth = 2.45f * stageScale
  2615	            canvas.drawLine(
  2616	                boxLeft + 5.3f * stageScale,
  2617	                boxTop + 11.8f * stageScale,
  2618	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  2619	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  2620	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  2621	            )
  2622	            canvas.drawLine(
  2623	                boxLeft + 9.1f * stageScale,
  2624	                boxTop + 15.8f * stageScale,
  2625	                boxLeft + 17.2f * stageScale,
  2626	                boxTop + 6.9f * stageScale,
  2627	                rememberMeCheckPaint
  2628	            )
  2629	            rememberMeCheckPaint.alpha = 255
  2630	        }
  2631	
  2632	        rememberMeTextPaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2633	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  2634	        rememberMeTextPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  2635	        val metrics = rememberMeTextPaint.fontMetrics
  2636	        val baselineY = (boxTop + boxBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2637	        canvas.drawText(
  2638	            MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT,
  2639	            boxRight + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_LABEL_GAP * stageScale,
  2640	            baselineY,
  2641	            rememberMeTextPaint
  2642	        )
  2643	        rememberMeTextPaint.alpha = 255
  2644	    }

  2646	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  2647	        canvas: Canvas,
  2648	        centerX: Float,
  2649	        topY: Float,
  2650	        stageLeft: Float,
  2651	        stageTop: Float,
  2652	        stageScale: Float,
  2653	        alpha: Float
  2654	    ) {
  2655	        val chevronAlpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (pressedBackChevron) 0.68f else 0.92f
  2656	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2657	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale
  2658	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * stageScale
  2659	        val halfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  2660	
  2661	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (chevronAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  2662	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2663	        canvas.drawLine(center - halfWidth, top, center, bottom, backChevronPaint)
  2664	        canvas.drawLine(center, bottom, center + halfWidth, top, backChevronPaint)
  2665	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  2666	    }
  2667	
  2668	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  2669	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  2670	    }
  2671	
  2672	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(
  2673	        source: Bitmap,
  2674	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean = false
  2675	    ): Bitmap {
  2676	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2677	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2678	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2679	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2680	
  2681	        val mask = createAvatarCharacterMask(pixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2682	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2683	            if (mask[index]) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  2684	        }
  2685	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2686	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2687	        }
  2688	    }
  2689	
  2690	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  2691	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  2692	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  2693	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  2694	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2695	
  2696	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  2697	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  2698	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  2699	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  2700	
  2701	        val outputPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  2702	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  2703	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  2704	            val dx = x - centerX
  2705	            val dy = y - centerY
  2706	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared) {
  2707	                Color.TRANSPARENT
  2708	            } else {
  2709	                pixels[index]
  2710	            }
  2711	        }
  2712	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  2713	            setPixels(outputPixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  2714	        }
  2715	    }
  2716	
  2717	    private fun createAvatarCharacterMask(
  2718	        pixels: IntArray,
  2719	        bitmapWidth: Int,
  2720	        bitmapHeight: Int,
  2721	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2722	    ): BooleanArray {
  2723	        val coloredPixels = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2724	        pixels.forEachIndexed { index, pixel ->
  2725	            coloredPixels[index] = isColorfulAvatarPixel(pixel, includeDarkSaturatedPixels)
  2726	        }
  2727	
  2728	        val mask = BooleanArray(pixels.size)
  2729	        val outlineRadius = 7
  2730	        for (y in 0 until bitmapHeight) {
  2731	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  2732	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  2733	                if (!coloredPixels[index]) continue
  2734	
  2735	                mask[index] = true
  2736	                for (dy in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2737	                    val sampleY = y + dy
  2738	                    if (sampleY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  2739	                    for (dx in -outlineRadius..outlineRadius) {
  2740	                        val sampleX = x + dx
  2741	                        if (sampleX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  2742	                        val sampleIndex = sampleY * bitmapWidth + sampleX
  2743	                        val samplePixel = pixels[sampleIndex]
  2744	                        val sampleAlpha = Color.alpha(samplePixel)
  2745	                        val sampleMax = maxOf(
  2746	                            Color.red(samplePixel),
  2747	                            Color.green(samplePixel),
  2748	                            Color.blue(samplePixel)
  2749	                        )
  2750	                        if (sampleAlpha > 16 && sampleMax <= 44) {
  2751	                            mask[sampleIndex] = true
  2752	                        }
  2753	                    }
  2754	                }
  2755	            }
  2756	        }
  2757	        return mask
  2758	    }
  2759	
  2760	    private fun isColorfulAvatarPixel(
  2761	        pixel: Int,
  2762	        includeDarkSaturatedPixels: Boolean
  2763	    ): Boolean {
  2764	        val alpha = Color.alpha(pixel)
  2765	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  2766	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  2767	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  2768	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  2769	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  2770	        if (alpha <= 16) return false
  2771	
  2772	        val chroma = channelMax - channelMin
  2773	        return (channelMax > 48 && chroma > 22) ||
  2774	            (includeDarkSaturatedPixels && channelMax > 36 && chroma > 14)
  2775	    }
  2776	
  2777	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  2778	        return when {
  2779	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  2780	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  2781	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  2782	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  2783	            else -> null
  2784	        }
  2785	    }
  2786	
  2787	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  2788	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  2789	    }

  2792	        return bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis) >= 1f
  2793	    }

  2795	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  2796	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  2797	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  2798	
  2799	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  2800	
  2801	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2802	            val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  2803	            val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  2804	            val centerY = stage.top + (portraitAvatarTop() + avatarSize / 2f) * stage.scale
  2805	            val radius = avatarSize * stage.scale / 2f
  2806	            val dx = x - centerX
  2807	            val dy = y - centerY
  2808	            return if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius) {
  2809	                FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  2810	            } else {
  2811	                null
  2812	            }
  2813	        }
  2814	
  2815	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  2816	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  2817	            val bounds = target.bounds
  2818	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  2819	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  2820	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  2821	            val dx = x - centerX
  2822	            val dy = y - centerY
  2823	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  2824	        }?.profile
  2825	    }

  2827	    private fun settledCreateAccountPromptHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  2828	        if (!accountPromptCanOpen(MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount)) return false

  2836	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_WIDTH
  2837	            )
  2838	        }

  2842	        val halfWidth = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  2843	        val halfHeight = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f

  2845	            y in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)

  3067	                (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stage.scale

  3071	        mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown).forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
  3072	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  3073	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  3074	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale

  3076	        }
  3077	        return null
  3078	    }

  3103	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3104	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  3105	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  3106	
  3107	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  3108	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  3109	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  3110	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  3111	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  3112	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  3113	        }
  3114	
  3115	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  3116	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  3117	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  3118	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3119	        }
  3120	        return true
  3121	    }

  3132	            mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
  3133	            stage
  3134	        )
  3135	    }
  3136	
  3137	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  3138	        if (mockDragReturnInProgress || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  3139	
  3140	        val upwardDragY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  3141	        if (upwardDragY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  3142	
  3143	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  3144	        return upwardDragY > abs(dragX)
  3145	    }
  3146	
  3147	    private fun mockBackDragThresholdPx(): Float {
  3148	        val stageThreshold = currentStageMetrics()?.let { stage ->
  3149	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  3150	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  3151	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  3152	    }

  3154	    private fun mockSignInFieldContains(
  3155	        pointerX: Float,

  3165	        val bottom = stage.top + (fieldY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stage.scale

  3167	    }

  3258	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselBounds(
  3259	        columnX: Float,
  3260	        firstFieldY: Float,
  3261	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  3262	    ): Bounds {
  3263	        val centerX = columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f
  3264	        val contentTop = firstFieldY
  3265	        val contentBottom = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h
  3266	        val contentCenterY = (contentTop + contentBottom) / 2f
  3267	        val chevronSpan = avatarBounds.w +
  3268	            (MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH / 2f) * 2f
  3269	        val side = maxOf(chevronSpan, contentBottom - contentTop)
  3270	        return Bounds(
  3271	            centerX - side / 2f,
  3272	            contentCenterY - side / 2f,
  3273	            side,
  3274	            side
  3275	        )
  3276	    }

  3297	    private fun mockCreateAvatarChevronContains(
  3298	        pointerX: Float,
  3299	        pointerY: Float,
  3300	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  3301	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  3302	        stage: StageMetrics
  3303	    ): Boolean {

  3305	        val centerY = stage.top + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stage.scale

  3309	            pointerY in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)

  3312	    private fun focusMockSignInField(field: MockSignInField) {
  3313	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = field
  3314	        mockInputDimActiveField = field
  3315	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  3316	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  3317	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  3318	        if (focusedMockField != field) {
  3319	            activeComposingText = ""
  3320	            focusedMockField = field
  3321	            restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  3322	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  3323	        }
  3324	        requestFocus()
  3325	        post {
  3326	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  3327	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)
  3328	            inputMethodManager?.showSoftInput(this, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
  3329	        }
  3330	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3331	    }

  3333	    private fun clearMockSignInFieldFocus() {
  3334	        if (focusedMockField == null) return
  3335	        activeComposingText = ""
  3336	        focusedMockField = null
  3337	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  3338	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  3339	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  3340	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  3341	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  3342	        val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  3343	        inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  3344	        clearFocus()
  3345	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3346	    }

  3571	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {

  3573	        val viewportCount = if (mockDropdownUsesDateViewport(dropdown)) {
  3574	            MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_VISIBLE_OPTION_COUNT

  3579	    }

  3581	    private fun mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  3582	        return mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size > mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  3583	    }
  3584	
  3585	    private fun mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3586	        return (mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  3587	    }
  3588	
  3589	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  3590	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)
  3591	    }
  3592	
  3593	    private fun setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown, offset: Int) {
  3594	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = offset.coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  3595	    }
  3596	
  3597	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown: MockDropdown): List<String> {
  3598	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  3599	        val offset = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown)
  3600	        return options.drop(offset).take(mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown))
  3601	    }
  3602	
  3603	    private fun prepareMockDropdownScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown) {
  3604	        if (!mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3605	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, 0)
  3606	            return
  3607	        }
  3608	
  3609	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))

  3748	    private fun mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress(): Float {
  3749	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return if (
  3750	            forgotPasswordSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3751	        ) {
  3752	            1f
  3753	        } else {
  3754	            0f
  3755	        }
  3756	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  3757	        return (elapsed / MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  3758	    }

  3765	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return
  3766	        if (forgotPasswordSubmissionState != MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  3767	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  3768	            return
  3769	        }
  3770	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS) return
  3771	
  3772	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  3773	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null

  3798	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = nextIndex
  3799	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  3800	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  3801	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  3802	    }

  3911

  3914	            mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  3915	        }

  3953	    private fun mockLandscapeInputLiftTarget(): Float {
  3954	        if (width <= height) return 0f
  3955	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  3956	        return if (
  3957	            focusedMockField != null ||
  3958	            expandedMockDropdown != null ||
  3959	            isSoftKeyboardVisible()
  3960	        ) {
  3961	            1f
  3962	        } else {
  3963	            0f
  3964	        }
  3965	    }
  3966	
  3967	    private fun mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale: Float): Float {
  3968	        if (width <= height || mockSignInStartMillis == null) return 0f
  3969	        val liftMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress)
  3970	        return MOCK_FORM_LANDSCAPE_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  3971	    }
  3972	
  3973	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  3974	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  3975	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  3976	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  3977	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  3978	        }
  3979	
  3980	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  3981	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  3982	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  3983	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  3984	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  3985	    }
  3986	
  3987	    private fun mockInputDimTarget(): Float {
  3988	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  3989	        if (expandedMockDropdown != null) return 1f
  3990	        val awaitingField = mockInputDimAwaitingField ?: return 0f
  3991	        return if (focusedMockField == awaitingField) 1f else 0f
  3992	    }

  3994	    private fun mockFormFocusTarget(): Float {

  3998	        }
  3999	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return 0f
  4000	        return if (
  4001	            focusedMockField != null ||
  4002	            activeMockFields().any { mockSignInFieldText(it).isNotEmpty() }
  4003	        ) {
  4004	            1f
  4005	        } else {
  4006	            0f
  4007	        }
  4008	    }

  4018	            -(LOGO_FINAL_TOP + LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT + 64f) * liftMotion

  4072	    private fun setMockSignInFieldText(field: MockSignInField, text: String) {

  4074	        when (field) {

  4076	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText = limitedText
  4077	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> confirmPasswordText = limitedText
  4078	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText = limitedText
  4079	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText = limitedText
  4080	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText = limitedText
  4081	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText = limitedText

  4083	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword && field == MockSignInField.Email) {
  4084	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4085	        }
  4086	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  4087	        if (mockInputDimAwaitingField == field) {
  4088	            mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  4089	        }
  4090	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  4091	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  4092	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  4093	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4094	    }

  4096	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  4097	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4098	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  4099	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  4100	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4101	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  4102	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4103	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  4104	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  4105	        activeComposingText = ""
  4106	        return true
  4107	    }
  4108	
  4109	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  4110	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4111	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  4112	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4113	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  4114	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  4115	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  4116	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  4117	        activeComposingText = composingText
  4118	        return true
  4119	    }
  4120	
  4121	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  4122	        activeComposingText = ""
  4123	        return true
  4124	    }
  4125	
  4126	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  4127	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  4128	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  4129	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  4130	            activeComposingText = ""
  4131	            return true
  4132	        }
  4133	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  4134	        activeComposingText = ""
  4135	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  4136	        return true
  4137	    }
  4138	
  4139	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  4140	        activeComposingText = ""
  4141	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  4142	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  4143	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  4144	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  4145	        } else if (
  4146	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  4147	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  4148	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  4149	        ) {
  4150	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  4151	        } else {
  4152	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  4153	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  4154	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  4155	        }
  4156	        return true
  4157	    }
  4158	
  4159	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  4160	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  4161	        return when (keyCode) {
  4162	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  4163	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  4164	            else -> {
  4165	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  4166	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  4167	            }
  4168	        }
  4169	    }
  4170	
  4171	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  4172	        return buildString {
  4173	            input.forEach { character ->
  4174	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  4175	                    append(character)
  4176	                }
  4177	            }
  4178	        }
  4179	    }
  4180	
  4181	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  4182	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  4183	
  4184	        var startIndex = 0
  4185	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  4186	            startIndex += 1
  4187	        }
  4188	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  4189	    }
  4190	
  4191	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  4192	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  4193	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  4194	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  4195	                lines += ""
  4196	            }
  4197	
  4198	            var currentLine = ""
  4199	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  4200	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  4201	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  4202	                .forEach { word ->
  4203	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  4204	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  4205	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  4206	                    } else {
  4207	                        lines += currentLine
  4208	                        currentLine = word
  4209	                    }
  4210	                }
  4211	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  4212	                lines += currentLine
  4213	            }
  4214	        }
  4215	        return lines
  4216	    }
  4217	
  4218	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  4219	        return when {
  4220	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  4221	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  4222	            else -> current
  4223	        }
  4224	    }

  4226	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
  4227	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
  4228	
  4229	        val standardScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  4230	        val portraitScale = width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
  4231	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  4232	        val stageScale = lerpFloat(standardScale, portraitScale, portraitAmount)
  4233	        val standardTop = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * standardScale) / 2f
  4234	        return StageMetrics(
  4235	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  4236	            top = lerpFloat(standardTop, 0f, portraitAmount) +
  4237	                mockAuthOpenStageLiftY(stageScale) +
  4238	                mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  4239	            scale = stageScale
  4240	        )
  4241	    }

  4435	    private fun switchMockAccountFlow(flow: MockAccountFlow) {
  4436	        if (
  4437	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  4438	            activeMockFlow == flow ||
  4439	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  4440	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()

  4449	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  4450	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  4451	        pressedMockField = null
  4452	        pressedRememberMe = false
  4453	        pressedBackChevron = false

  4459	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4460	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null

  4463	        if (flow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {

  4562	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  4563	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  4564	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  4565	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4566	        return lerpFloat(
  4567	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  4568	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  4569	            progress
  4570	        )
  4571	    }

  4598	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  4599	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  4600	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  4601	            .roundToInt()
  4602	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  4603	            .toLong()
  4604	    }
  4605	
  4606	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  4607	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  4608	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  4609	
  4610	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  4611	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  4612	            activeMockFlow = null
  4613	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  4614	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  4615	            pressedMockField = null
  4616	            pressedRememberMe = false
  4617	            pressedBackChevron = false
  4618	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4619	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  4620	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4621	            pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null

  4636	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {
  4637	        override fun commitText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  4638	            return appendMockSignInInput(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  4639	        }
  4640	
  4641	        override fun setComposingText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  4642	            return replaceMockSignInComposingText(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  4643	        }
  4644	
  4645	        override fun finishComposingText(): Boolean {
  4646	            return finishMockSignInComposingText()
  4647	        }
  4648	
  4649	        override fun deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  4650	            return deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength)
  4651	        }
  4652	
  4653	        override fun deleteSurroundingTextInCodePoints(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  4654	            return deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength, afterLength)
  4655	        }
  4656	
  4657	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4658	            val text = focusedMockField?.let(::mockSignInFieldText).orEmpty()
  4659	            return text.takeLast(n.coerceAtLeast(0))
  4660	        }
  4661	
  4662	        override fun getTextAfterCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4663	            return ""
  4664	        }
  4665	
  4666	        override fun getSelectedText(flags: Int): CharSequence {
  4667	            return ""
  4668	        }
  4669	
  4670	        override fun performEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  4671	            return handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode)
  4672	        }
  4673	
  4674	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  4675	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  4676	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  4677	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  4678	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  4679	                else -> {
  4680	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  4681	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  4682	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  4683	                    } else {
  4684	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  4685	                    }
  4686	                }
  4687	            }
  4688	        }
  4689	    }
  4690	}

  4846	private inline fun <reified T : Enum<T>> enumValueOrNull(name: String): T? {
  4847	    return runCatching { enumValueOf<T>(name) }.getOrNull()
  4848	}
  4849	
  4850	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  4851	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  4852	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4853	}
  4854	
  4855	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4856	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  4857	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4858	}
  4859	
  4860	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4861	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  4862	}
  4863	
  4864	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  4865	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  4866	}
  4867	
  4868	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  4869	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4870	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  4871	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  4872	    return base + bounce
  4873	}
  4874	
  4875	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  4876	
  4877	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  4878	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  4879	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  4880	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  4881	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  4882	)
  4883	
  4884	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  4885	    x = bounds.x,
  4886	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  4887	    w = bounds.w,
  4888	    h = bounds.h
  4889	)
  4890	
  4891	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  4892	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  4893	    return Color.argb(
  4894	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  4895	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  4896	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  4897	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  4898	    )
  4899	}
  4900	
  4901	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  4902	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  4903	}
  4904	
  4905	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  4906	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  4907	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  4908	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  4909	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  4910	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  4911	    return output
  4912	}
  4913	
  4914	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  4915	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  4916	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  4917	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  4918	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  4919	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  4920	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  4921	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  4922	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  4923	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  4924	    return output
  4925	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	### Standard tablet emulator
    32	
    33	Use the guarded launcher for the persistent low-load QA profile:
    34	
    35	```bash
    36	./start_cinerific_android_standard.sh
    37	```
    38	
    39	The launcher requests and verifies `1280x800` at `160 dpi`, two guest CPU cores,
    40	host GPU acceleration, and the repository-owned rounded tablet bezel before
    41	opening Cinerific. It refuses to continue if the running AVD does not match.
    42	Emulator logs are written to
    43	`~/Library/Logs/Cinerific/standard-emulator.log`.
    44	
    45	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    46	
    47	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    48	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    49	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.

============================================================
FILE: start_cinerific_android_standard.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	set -Eeuo pipefail
     3	
     4	ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
     5	SDK="${ANDROID_HOME:-${ANDROID_SDK_ROOT:-$HOME/Library/Android/sdk}}"
     6	EMU="${SDK}/emulator/emulator"
     7	ADB="${SDK}/platform-tools/adb"
     8	AVD="Cinerific_Standard_Tablet"
     9	AVD_CONFIG="$HOME/.android/avd/${AVD}.avd/config.ini"
    10	DEVICE="emulator-5554"
    11	PORT="5554"
    12	APP_PACKAGE="com.prahlin.cinerific"
    13	APP_ACTIVITY="${APP_PACKAGE}/.MainActivity"
    14	EXPECTED_SIZE="1280x800"
    15	EXPECTED_DENSITY="160"
    16	EXPECTED_CORES="2"
    17	EXPECTED_SKIN="cinerific_pixel_tablet_1280"
    18	BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS="${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:-180}"
    19	LOG_DIR="$HOME/Library/Logs/Cinerific"
    20	LOG="$LOG_DIR/standard-emulator.log"
    21	ERROR_LOG="$LOG_DIR/standard-emulator.err.log"
    22	LAUNCHD_LABEL="com.cinerific.standardavd"
    23	LAUNCHD_PLIST="$ROOT/launchd/${LAUNCHD_LABEL}.plist"
    24	
    25	fail() {
    26	  printf 'ERROR: %s\n' "$*" >&2
    27	  exit 1
    28	}
    29	
    30	[[ -x "$EMU" ]] || fail "Android emulator not found at $EMU"
    31	[[ -x "$ADB" ]] || fail "adb not found at $ADB"
    32	[[ -f "$LAUNCHD_PLIST" ]] || fail "LaunchAgent not found at $LAUNCHD_PLIST"
    33	[[ -f "$AVD_CONFIG" ]] || fail "AVD configuration not found at $AVD_CONFIG"
    34	"$EMU" -list-avds | grep -qx "$AVD" || fail "AVD $AVD was not found"
    35	
    36	config_skin="$(sed -n 's/^skin.name=//p' "$AVD_CONFIG")"
    37	config_frame="$(sed -n 's/^showDeviceFrame=//p' "$AVD_CONFIG")"
    38	[[ "$config_skin" == "$EXPECTED_SKIN" ]] || fail "Expected skin $EXPECTED_SKIN, got $config_skin"
    39	[[ "$config_frame" == "yes" ]] || fail "Device frame is not enabled in $AVD_CONFIG"
    40	
    41	mkdir -p "$LOG_DIR"
    42	
    43	device_is_connected() {
    44	  "$ADB" devices | awk -v device="$DEVICE" \
    45	    '$1 == device && $2 == "device" { found = 1 } END { exit !found }'
    46	}
    47	
    48	if device_is_connected; then
    49	  actual_avd="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" emu avd name 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r' | sed -n '1p')"
    50	  [[ "$actual_avd" == "$AVD" ]] || fail "$DEVICE is running $actual_avd, not $AVD"
    51	  printf '%s is already running.\n' "$AVD"
    52	else
    53	  printf 'Starting %s with the low-load QA profile...\n' "$AVD"
    54	  launchctl bootout "gui/$(id -u)/${LAUNCHD_LABEL}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    55	  : > "$LOG"
    56	  : > "$ERROR_LOG"
    57	  launchctl bootstrap "gui/$(id -u)" "$LAUNCHD_PLIST"
    58	fi
    59	
    60	deadline=$((SECONDS + BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS))
    61	until device_is_connected; do
    62	  (( SECONDS < deadline )) || fail "Emulator did not connect within ${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s; see $LOG"
    63	  sleep 2
    64	done
    65	
    66	until [[ "$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell getprop sys.boot_completed 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r')" == "1" ]]; do
    67	  (( SECONDS < deadline )) || fail "Android did not finish booting within ${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s; see $LOG"
    68	  sleep 2
    69	done
    70	
    71	actual_avd="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" emu avd name 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r' | sed -n '1p')"
    72	actual_size="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell wm size | sed -n 's/^Physical size: //p' | tr -d '\r')"
    73	actual_density="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell wm density | sed -n 's/^Physical density: //p' | tr -d '\r')"
    74	actual_cores="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell 'grep -c "^processor" /proc/cpuinfo' | tr -d '\r')"
    75	
    76	[[ "$actual_avd" == "$AVD" ]] || fail "Expected $AVD, got $actual_avd"
    77	[[ "$actual_size" == "$EXPECTED_SIZE" ]] || fail "Expected $EXPECTED_SIZE, got $actual_size"
    78	[[ "$actual_density" == "$EXPECTED_DENSITY" ]] || fail "Expected density $EXPECTED_DENSITY, got $actual_density"
    79	[[ "$actual_cores" == "$EXPECTED_CORES" ]] || fail "Expected $EXPECTED_CORES cores, got $actual_cores"
    80	
    81	sleep 10
    82	device_is_connected || fail "Emulator disconnected after boot; see $LOG"
    83	
    84	if "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell pm path "$APP_PACKAGE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    85	  "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell am force-stop "$APP_PACKAGE"
    86	  "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell am start -n "$APP_ACTIVITY" >/dev/null
    87	  printf 'Cinerific launched on %s.\n' "$DEVICE"
    88	else
    89	  printf 'Cinerific is not installed on %s; emulator verification still passed.\n' "$DEVICE"
    90	fi
    91	
    92	printf 'Verified: %s @ %s dpi, %s cores, host GPU.\n' \
    93	  "$actual_size" "$actual_density" "$actual_cores"
    94	printf 'Emulator log: %s\n' "$LOG"


============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/cinerific_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Cinerific project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Cinerific Android PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you don't understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	
    28	
    29	============================================================
    30	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    31	============================================================
    32	
    33	This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    34	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    35	
    36	The snapshot intentionally ignores:
    37	- .git
    38	- .gradle
    39	- .idea
    40	- app/build
    41	- build
    42	- .kotlin
    43	- .tmp
    44	- .cache
    45	- .DS_Store
    46	- local.properties
    47	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    48	- *.jks
    49	- *.keystore
    50	
    51	Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    52	
    53	
    54	============================================================
    55	PROJECT FILE TREE
    56	============================================================
    57	
    58	EOF
    59	}
    60	
    61	print_file() {
    62	  local file="$1"
    63	
    64	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    65	    {
    66	      echo ""
    67	      echo ""
    68	      echo "============================================================"
    69	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    70	      echo "============================================================"
    71	      echo ""
    72	      nl -ba "$file"
    73	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	  fi
    75	}
    76	
    77	print_binary_inventory() {
    78	  local title="$1"
    79	  local dir="$2"
    80	
    81	  {
    82	    echo ""
    83	    echo ""
    84	    echo "============================================================"
    85	    echo "$title"
    86	    echo "============================================================"
    87	    echo ""
    88	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    89	
    90	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
    91	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	    return
    93	  fi
    94	
    95	  find "$dir" -type f \
    96	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
    97	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
    98	    ! -name "*.jks" \
    99	    ! -name "*.keystore" \
   100	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   101	    local size
   102	    local modified
   103	    size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   104	    modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   105	    printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   106	  done
   107	}
   108	
   109	write_snapshot() {
   110	  write_header
   111	
   112	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   113	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   114	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.gradle" -prune -o \
   115	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.idea" -prune -o \
   116	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build" -prune -o \
   117	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   118	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.kotlin" -prune -o \
   119	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.tmp" -prune -o \
   120	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   121	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   122	    -name "local.properties" -prune -o \
   123	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   124	    -name "*.jks" -prune -o \
   125	    -name "*.keystore" -prune -o \
   126	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   127	
   128	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   129	
   130	
   131	============================================================
   132	FILE CONTENTS — ANDROID SOURCE
   133	============================================================
   134	
   135	EOF
   136	
   137	  for file in \
   138	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml" \
   139	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt" \
   140	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificApp.kt" \
   141	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificIntroView.kt" \
   142	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/theme/Theme.kt" \
   143	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml" \
   144	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/themes.xml" \
   145	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-night/themes.xml" \
   146	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-v31/themes.xml" \
   147	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/splash_transparent.xml" \
   148	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_background.xml" \
   149	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_foreground.xml" \
   150	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml" \
   151	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher_round.xml"
   152	  do
   153	    print_file "$file"
   154	  done
   155	
   156	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   157	
   158	
   159	============================================================
   160	FILE CONTENTS — ROOT / TOOLING
   161	============================================================
   162	
   163	EOF
   164	
   165	  for file in \
   166	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   167	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/copilot-instructions.md" \
   168	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/mcp.json" \
   169	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/tasks.json" \
   170	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   171	    "$PROJECT_DIR/build.gradle.kts" \
   172	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build.gradle.kts" \
   173	    "$PROJECT_DIR/settings.gradle.kts" \
   174	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle.properties" \
   175	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties" \
   176	    "$PROJECT_DIR/launchd/com.cinerific.standardavd.plist" \
   177	    "$PROJECT_DIR/start_cinerific_android_standard.sh" \
   178	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   179	  do
   180	    print_file "$file"
   181	  done
   182	
   183	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — DRAWABLE-NODPI" "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi"
   184	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — EMULATOR SKINS" "$PROJECT_DIR/emulator/skins"
   185	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / SCREENSHOT INVENTORY" "$PROJECT_DIR/screenshots"
   186	
   187	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   188	
   189	
   190	============================================================
   191	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   192	============================================================
   193	
   194	The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
   195	- .git
   196	- .gradle
   197	- .idea
   198	- app/build
   199	- build
   200	- .kotlin
   201	- .tmp
   202	- .cache
   203	- .DS_Store
   204	- local.properties
   205	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   206	- *.jks
   207	- *.keystore
   208	
   209	The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
   210	
   211	EOF
   212	}
   213	
   214	write_snapshot
   215	
   216	echo "Done."
   217	echo ""
   218	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   219	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   220	echo ""
   221	echo "Open it with:"
   222	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
